Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the stereochemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 818 and the convergence rate is O(162/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on stereochemistry structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 818. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 162. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on stereochemistry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 524. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 813 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 524 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the stereochemistry structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 813. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
